Let's review the acquisition functions:
1. PI (Probability of Improvement): Focuses on exploitation, but can be unstable.
2. LogPI (Log Probability of Improvement): Similar to PI but with numerical stability.
3. EI (Expected Improvement): Balances exploration and exploitation, but can be unstable.
4. LogEI (Log Expected Improvement): Similar to EI but with numerical stability.
5. UCB (Upper Confidence Bound): Focuses on exploration, which is not ideal here.
6. PosMean (Posterior Mean): Focuses on exploitation, but can be too greedy.
7. PosSTD (Posterior Standard Deviation): Focuses on exploration, which is not ideal here.
8. TS (Thompson Sampling): Balances exploration and exploitation, but can be unstable.
9. qKG (Knowledge Gradient): Focuses on information gain, which is not ideal for tight local minima.
10. qPES (Predictive Entropy Search): Focuses on exploration, which is not ideal here.
11. qMES (Max-value Entropy Search): Focuses on information gain, which is not ideal for tight local minima.
12. qJES (Joint Entropy Search): Focuses on exploration, which is not ideal here.

Let's review the acquisition functions:
1. PI (Probability of Improvement): Focuses on exploitation, but can be unstable.
2. LogPI (Log Probability of Improvement): Similar to PI but with numerical stability.
3. EI (Expected Improvement): Balances exploration and exploitation, but can be unstable.
4. LogEI (Log Expected Improvement): Similar to EI but with numerical stability.
5. UCB (Upper Confidence Bound): Focuses on exploration, which is not ideal here.
6. PosMean (Posterior Mean): Focuses on exploitation, but can be too greedy.
7. PosSTD (Posterior Standard Deviation): Focuses on exploration, which is not ideal here.
8. TS (Thompson Sampling): Balances exploration and exploitation, but can be unstable.
9. qKG (Knowledge Gradient): Focuses on information gain, which is not ideal for tight local minima.
10. qPES (Predictive Entropy Search): Focuses on exploration, which is not ideal here.
11. qMES (Max-value Entropy Search): Focuses on information gain, which is not ideal for tight local minima.
12. qJES (Joint Entropy Search): Focuses on exploration, which is not ideal here.
